# Quest-Ear NPC corpus — translation review

354 source strings × 24 new languages. Generated by scripts/i18n/review.mjs. Eyeball each language below.

Languages: Catalan (ca), Romanian (ro), Portuguese (European) (pt-PT), Dutch (nl), Norwegian (no), Swedish (sv), Danish (da), Finnish (fi), Lithuanian (lt), Czech (cs), Slovak (sk), Slovenian (sl), Croatian (hr), Serbian (sr), Bulgarian (bg), Ukrainian (uk), Greek (el), Hebrew (he), Punjabi (Shahmukhi) (pa-Arab), Punjabi (Gurmukhi) (pa-Guru), Nepali (ne), Malay (ms), Swahili (sw), Cantonese (Traditional) (yue-Hant-HK)

## Punjabi (Gurmukhi) (`pa-Guru`)

| English | pa-Guru |
|---|---|
| A mix please! | ਇੱਕ ਮਿਲਾਵਟ, ਜੀ! |
| A rose for you? | ਤੁਹਾਡੇ ਲਈ ਇੱਕ ਗੁਲਾਬ? |
| Absolutely! | ਬਿਲਕੁਲ! |
| Açaí smoothie? | ਅਕਾਈ ਸਮੂਦੀ? |
| Afraid of heights. | ਮੈਨੂੰ ਉਚਾਈ ਤੋਂ ਡਰ ਲੱਗਦਾ ਹੈ। |
| Airport? Midtown? | ਹਵਾਈ ਅੱਡਾ? ਸ਼ਹਿਰ ਦੇ ਵਿਚਕਾਰ? |
| All the way up! | ਬਿਲਕੁਲ ਉੱਪਰ ਤੱਕ! |
| Allergic. | ਮੈਨੂੰ ਐਲਰਜੀ ਹੈ। |
| Allergies. | ਐਲਰਜੀਆਂ। |
| Alleyways whisper old gossip. | ਗਲੀਆਂ ਪੁਰਾਣੀਆਂ ਗੱਲਾਂ ਘੁਸਰ-ਮੁਸਰ ਕਰਦੀਆਂ ਨੇ। |
| Already ate. | ਮੈਂ ਪਹਿਲਾਂ ਹੀ ਖਾ ਲਿਆ ਹੈ। |
| An apple a day is enough. | ਦਿਨ ਦਾ ਇੱਕ ਸੇਬ ਕਾਫ਼ੀ ਹੈ। |
| Apples, just picked! | ਸੇਬ, ਹੁਣੇ ਹੀ ਤੋੜੇ ਨੇ! |
| Awnings are building eyelids. | ਛੱਜੇ ਇਮਾਰਤਾਂ ਦੀਆਂ ਪਲਕਾਂ ਨੇ। |
| Banana bunch? | ਕੇਲਿਆਂ ਦਾ ਇੱਕ ਗੁੱਛਾ? |
| Beautiful bouquet? | ਇੱਕ ਖੂਬਸੂਰਤ ਗੁਲਦਸਤਾ? |
| Been there. | ਮੈਂ ਉੱਥੇ ਜਾ ਚੁੱਕਿਆ ਹਾਂ। |
| Benches judge everyone. | ਬੈਂਚ ਹਰ ਕਿਸੇ ਨੂੰ ਪਰਖਦੇ ਨੇ। |
| Berry blast? | ਬੇਰੀਆਂ ਦਾ ਧਮਾਕਾ? |
| Best coffee on the block! | ਮੁਹੱਲੇ ਦੀ ਸਭ ਤੋਂ ਵਧੀਆ ਕਾਫ਼ੀ! |
| Best dogs in the city! | ਸ਼ਹਿਰ ਦੇ ਸਭ ਤੋਂ ਵਧੀਆ ਹਾਟ ਡੌਗ! |
| Best pizza in New York! | ਨਿਊ ਯਾਰਕ ਦਾ ਸਭ ਤੋਂ ਵਧੀਆ ਪੀਜ਼ਾ! |
| Best pretzels in town! | ਸ਼ਹਿਰ ਦੇ ਸਭ ਤੋਂ ਵਧੀਆ ਪ੍ਰੈਟਜ਼ਲ! |
| Best views! | ਸਭ ਤੋਂ ਵਧੀਆ ਨਜ਼ਾਰੇ! |
| Bigger is better! | ਜਿੰਨਾ ਵੱਡਾ, ਓਨਾ ਵਧੀਆ! |

| So juicy! | 好多汁呀! |
| So trendy! | 好潮呀! |
| Soft pretzel, warm! | 鬆軟扭結餅，熱辣辣! |
| Sounds great! | 正喎! |
| Sounds intense. | 聽落好勁喎。 |
| Sounds refreshing! | 聽落好醒神呀! |
| Spoons are just tiny shovels. | 啲匙羹只係細鏟啫。 |
| Sports section? | 體育版呀? |
| Squirrels are spies. | 啲松鼠係間諜嚟㗎。 |
| Stage fright. | 怯場呀。 |
| Stairs go both ways. | 樓梯兩邊都通㗎。 |
| Stairs make me suspicious. | 樓梯令我覺得有古怪。 |
| Stairwells echo yesterday's footsteps. | 樓梯間迴響住尋日嘅腳步聲。 |
| Standing room only! | 得企位呀! |
| Steam vents are the city yawning. | 蒸氣口係城市喺度打喊露。 |
| Step on it! | 踩盡油門啦! |
| Stoops are front-row seats to life. | 門口嘅梯級係睇人生嘅頭排位。 |
| Stoplights blush at busy intersections. | 繁忙嘅路口紅綠燈會面紅。 |
| Storm drains sing at midnight. | 雨水渠喺半夜會唱歌。 |
| Street art is the city's diary. | 街頭藝術係城市嘅日記。 |
| Street signs are lost. | 啲路牌蕩失咗路。 |
| Strong scents bother me. | 啲味太濃會搞到我唔舒服。 |
| Subway grates breathe warm secrets. | 地鐵嘅鐵格喺度呼出溫暖嘅秘密。 |
| Subway rails dream of rivers. | 地鐵路軌發緊河流嘅夢。 |
| Subway rats have a king. | 地鐵啲老鼠有個王。 |
| Subway tunnels are the city dreaming. | 地鐵隧道係城市發緊嘅夢。 |
| Summer in a bite! | 一啖就食到夏天! |
| Sunday edition? | 星期日版呀? |
| Sunflowers! | 向日葵! |
| Sure thing! | 梗係得啦! |
| Sweet strawberries? | 甜士多啤梨呀? |
| Take me to the tropics! | 帶我去熱帶啦! |
| Taxi horns speak a secret language. | 的士嘅響號講緊一種秘密語言。 |
| Taxi! Where to? | 的士!去邊度呀? |
| Taxis are yellow submarines. | 的士係黃色嘅潛水艇。 |
| Taxis are yellow thoughts. | 的士係黃色嘅諗法。 |
| Thanks anyway. | 都係唔該晒。 |
| That's perfect! | 咁就最啱啦! |
| That's too much. | 咁太多喇。 |
| The moon is following me. | 月光跟住我。 |
| The thick one! | 要嗰條粗嘅! |
| They deserve it! | 佢哋抵㗎! |
| They wilt too fast. | 佢哋好快就謝㗎喇。 |
| They're lovely! | 好靚呀! |
| Thin crust, just right! | 薄底,啱啱好! |
| Thorns scare me. | 啲刺嚇親我。 |
| Tie me up! | 幫我綁一個! |
| Today's headlines! | 今日嘅頭條! |
| Too brainy for me. | 對我嚟講太燒腦喇。 |
| Too exotic for me. | 對我嚟講太古怪喇。 |
| Too expensive. | 太貴喇。 |
| Too full. | 太飽喇。 |
| Too knotty for me. | 對我嚟講太多結喇。 |
| Too messy to eat. | 食起上嚟太邋遢喇。 |
| Too pricey. | 太貴喇。 |
| Too sour. | 太酸喇。 |
| Too sweet for me. | 對我嚟講太甜喇。 |
| Too tall for my apartment. | 對我間屋嚟講太高喇。 |
| Traffic lights are the city's heartbeat. | 紅綠燈係城市嘅心跳。 |
| Traffic lights have feelings. | 紅綠燈有感情㗎。 |
| Trash cans are hungry. | 垃圾桶好肚餓㗎。 |
| Trees gossip about us. | 啲樹喺度講我哋是非。 |
| Tropical blend! | 熱帶混合！ |
| Tropical fruit? | 熱帶生果？ |
| Tulips today! | 今日有鬱金香！ |
| Twisted golden treat? | 扭埋一舊嘅金色小食？ |
| Two for five dollars! | 兩個五蚊！ |
| Two please! | 唔該兩個！ |
| Two slices for three! | 兩件三蚊！ |
| Waiting for summer fruit. | 等緊夏天嘅生果。 |
| Walls whisper at night. | 啲牆夜晚會細聲講嘢。 |
| Want a hot dog? | 要唔要熱狗？ |
| Want a slice? | 要唔要一件？ |
| Warm and chewy! | 暖笠笠又煙韌！ |
| Watching my carbs. | 我控制緊碳水。 |
| Water towers wear hats. | 啲水塔戴住帽。 |
| Watermelon cooler? | 西瓜冰飲？ |
| Watermelon slice? | 一片西瓜？ |
| Weather forecast? | 天氣預報？ |
| What a deal! | 好抵呀！ |
| What happened? | 發生咗咩事？ |
| What is happiness? | 咩係快樂？ |
| What's in season? | 而家咩當造？ |
| What's my future? | 我嘅未來會點？ |
| What's showing? | 做緊咩戲？ |
| What's the buzz? | 有咩新鮮事？ |
| Who won? | 邊個贏咗？ |
| Wifi passwords are modern poetry. | Wifi 密碼係現代詩嚟㗎。 |
| Window boxes are building jewelry. | 啲窗台花箱係大廈嘅首飾。 |
| With everything on it? | 全部料都加埋？ |
| Wrap it up! | 包起佢啦！ |
| Yellow energy! | 黃色能量！ |
| Yes please! | 好呀，唔該！ |
| Yes, healthy choice! | 係呀，健康之選！ |
| Yes, ice cold! | 係呀，凍冰冰！ |
| Yes, let's go! | 好呀，行啦！ |
| You bet! | 梗係啦！ |
| You read my mind! | 你估中咗我諗緊乜！ |
| You're right! | 你講得啱！ |
